### [148] [REGAL: A Registry-Driven Architecture for Deterministic Grounding of Agentic AI in Enterprise Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03018)
*Yuvraj Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REGAL是一个面向企业遥测数据的确定性AI代理架构，通过注册表驱动的方式将原始事件流转换为版本控制的可执行工具，解决LLM在企业私有数据上的上下文限制、本地语义概念和接口演化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工程组织产生大量异构遥测数据，但大型语言模型在企业私有遥测数据上实现自动化面临三大挑战：有限的模型上下文、本地定义的语义概念、以及不断演化的指标接口。需要一种架构方法来确保AI代理的确定性行为。

Method: REGAL采用显式架构方法：1) Medallion ELT管道生成可重放、语义压缩的Gold数据工件；2) 注册表驱动的编译层，从声明式指标定义合成MCP工具。注册表作为"代码即接口"层，确保工具规范与执行的一致性。

Result: 原型实现和案例研究验证了确定性接地的可行性，展示了其在延迟、令牌效率和运营治理方面的影响。该架构将确定性计算和语义编译提升为AI代理系统的首要设计原语。

Conclusion: REGAL系统化了企业LLM接地的架构模式，不提出新的学习算法，而是将确定性计算和语义编译提升为AI代理系统的首要设计原语，解决了企业环境中AI代理的实用性问题。

Abstract: Enterprise engineering organizations produce high-volume, heterogeneous telemetry from version control systems, CI/CD pipelines, issue trackers, and observability platforms. Large Language Models (LLMs) enable new forms of agentic automation, but grounding such agents on private telemetry raises three practical challenges: limited model context, locally defined semantic concepts, and evolving metric interfaces.
  We present REGAL, a registry-driven architecture for deterministic grounding of agentic AI systems in enterprise telemetry. REGAL adopts an explicitly architectural approach: deterministic telemetry computation is treated as a first-class primitive, and LLMs operate over a bounded, version-controlled action space rather than raw event streams.
  The architecture combines (1) a Medallion ELT pipeline that produces replayable, semantically compressed Gold artifacts, and (2) a registry-driven compilation layer that synthesizes Model Context Protocol (MCP) tools from declarative metric definitions. The registry functions as an "interface-as-code" layer, ensuring alignment between tool specification and execution, mitigating tool drift, and embedding governance policies directly at the semantic boundary.
  A prototype implementation and case study validate the feasibility of deterministic grounding and illustrate its implications for latency, token efficiency, and operational governance. This work systematizes an architectural pattern for enterprise LLM grounding; it does not propose new learning algorithms, but rather elevates deterministic computation and semantic compilation to first-class design primitives for agentic systems.

</details>


### [149] [TikZilla: Scaling Text-to-TikZ with High-Quality Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03072)
*Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员构建了更大更高质量的DaTikZ-V4数据集，并训练了TikZilla模型，通过两阶段SFT+RL方法显著提升了文本到TikZ代码生成的质量，在小模型规模下达到了与GPT-5相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到TikZ生成任务面临两个主要问题：1）现有数据集太小且噪声多，无法捕捉TikZ的复杂性；2）现有方法仅依赖监督微调，模型无法理解渲染图形的语义，导致循环、无关内容和空间关系错误等问题。

Method: 1）构建DaTikZ-V4数据集，比前代大4倍且质量更高，包含LLM生成的图形描述；2）训练TikZilla模型系列（3B和8B参数），采用两阶段流程：先监督微调，后强化学习；3）强化学习中使用通过逆向图形训练的图像编码器提供语义准确的奖励信号。

Result: 超过1000次人工评估显示：TikZilla在5分制上比基础模型提升1.5-2分，超越GPT-4o 0.5分，在基于图像的评估中与GPT-5表现相当，且模型规模小得多。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量数据集和结合SFT与RL的两阶段训练方法，可以在小模型规模下实现高质量的文本到TikZ代码生成，为科学图形生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to assist scientists across diverse workflows. A key challenge is generating high-quality figures from textual descriptions, often represented as TikZ programs that can be rendered as scientific images. Prior research has proposed a variety of datasets and modeling approaches for this task. However, existing datasets for Text-to-TikZ are too small and noisy to capture the complexity of TikZ, causing mismatches between text and rendered figures. Moreover, prior approaches rely solely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which does not expose the model to the rendered semantics of the figure, often resulting in errors such as looping, irrelevant content, and incorrect spatial relations. To address these issues, we construct DaTikZ-V4, a dataset more than four times larger and substantially higher in quality than DaTikZ-V3, enriched with LLM-generated figure descriptions. Using this dataset, we train TikZilla, a family of small open-source Qwen models (3B and 8B) with a two-stage pipeline of SFT followed by reinforcement learning (RL). For RL, we leverage an image encoder trained via inverse graphics to provide semantically faithful reward signals. Extensive human evaluations with over 1,000 judgments show that TikZilla improves by 1.5-2 points over its base models on a 5-point scale, surpasses GPT-4o by 0.5 points, and matches GPT-5 in the image-based evaluation, while operating at much smaller model sizes. Code, data, and models will be made available.

</details>


### [150] [RAPO: Expanding Exploration for LLM Agents via Retrieval-Augmented Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03078)
*Siwei Zhang,Yun Xiong,Xi Chen,Zi'an Jia,Renhong Huang,Jiarong Xu,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAPO框架，通过检索增强探索，在Agentic RL中结合离策略步骤级轨迹，提升LLM智能体的推理能力和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic RL方法主要依赖纯同策略范式进行探索，限制在智能体自身生成的输出范围内，无法发现新的推理视角。虽然近期研究尝试加入离策略信号，但通常使用完整的离策略轨迹进行轨迹级策略估计，忽略了智能体展开过程中细粒度的步骤级探索动态。

Method: 提出检索增强策略优化(RAPO)框架，包含两个阶段：1) 混合策略智能体展开：允许智能体持续推理检索到的离策略步骤级轨迹，动态扩展推理感知范围；2) 检索感知策略优化：通过检索奖励和重要性塑形校准策略梯度估计，稳定训练并优先考虑检索启发的探索。

Result: 在三个智能体推理任务的十四个数据集上，RAPO实现了平均+5.0%的性能提升，同时训练效率提高了1.2倍。

Conclusion: RAPO通过引入检索机制显式扩展Agentic RL中的探索，有效解决了现有方法探索受限的问题，在多个任务上取得了显著的性能提升和训练效率改进。

Abstract: Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) has shown remarkable potential in large language model-based (LLM) agents. These works can empower LLM agents to tackle complex tasks via multi-step, tool-integrated reasoning. However, an inherent limitation of existing Agentic RL methods is their reliance on a pure on-policy paradigm for exploration, restricting exploration to the agent's self-generated outputs and preventing the discovery of new reasoning perspectives for further improvement. While recent efforts incorporate auxiliary off-policy signals to enhance exploration, they typically utilize full off-policy trajectories for trajectory-level policy estimation, overlooking the necessity for the fine-grained, step-level exploratory dynamics within agentic rollout. In this paper, we revisit exploration in Agentic RL and propose Retrieval-Augmented Policy Optimization (RAPO), a novel RL framework that introduces retrieval to explicitly expand exploration during training. To achieve this, we decompose the Agentic RL training process into two phases: (i) Hybrid-policy Agentic Rollout, and (ii) Retrieval-aware Policy Optimization. Specifically, we propose a Hybrid-policy Agentic Rollout strategy, which allows the agents to continuously reason over the retrieved off-policy step-level traces. It dynamically extends the reasoning receptive field of agents, enabling broader exploration conditioned on external behaviors. Subsequently, we introduce the Retrieval-aware Policy Optimization mechanism, which calibrates the policy gradient estimation with retrieval reward and importance shaping, stabilizing training and prioritizing retrieval-illuminating exploration. Extensive experiments show that RAPO achieves an +5.0% average gain on fourteen datasets across three agentic reasoning tasks, while delivering 1.2x faster training efficiency.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Factual Correctness: Mitigating Preference-Inconsistent Explanations in Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03080)
*Chengkai Wang,Baisong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PURE框架通过选择与用户偏好一致的多跳推理路径，减少推荐解释中的偏好不一致问题，同时保持事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐解释系统虽然能生成事实正确的解释，但经常使用与用户历史偏好冲突的属性来推荐物品，导致逻辑有效但缺乏说服力的解释，而标准幻觉或忠实度指标无法检测这种偏好不一致问题。

Method: PURE采用"选择-生成"范式，在证据选择阶段干预，选择既事实可靠又与用户偏好结构对齐的紧凑多跳物品中心推理路径，考虑用户意图、特异性和多样性来抑制通用、弱个性化的证据，然后通过保持关系约束的结构感知提示将选定证据注入LLM生成过程。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，PURE能持续减少偏好不一致解释和事实幻觉，同时保持有竞争力的推荐准确性、解释质量和推理效率。

Conclusion: 可信的解释不仅需要事实正确性，还需要与用户偏好对齐的合理性，PURE框架通过偏好感知推理解决了这一关键问题。

Abstract: LLM-based explainable recommenders can produce fluent explanations that are factually correct, yet still justify items using attributes that conflict with a user's historical preferences. Such preference-inconsistent explanations yield logically valid but unconvincing reasoning and are largely missed by standard hallucination or faithfulness metrics. We formalize this failure mode and propose PURE, a preference-aware reasoning framework following a select-then-generate paradigm. Instead of only improving generation, PURE intervenes in evidence selection, it selects a compact set of multi-hop item-centric reasoning paths that are both factually grounded and aligned with user preference structure, guided by user intent, specificity, and diversity to suppress generic, weakly personalized evidence. The selected evidence is then injected into LLM generation via structure-aware prompting that preserves relational constraints. To measure preference inconsistency, we introduce a feature-level, user-centric evaluation metric that reveals misalignment overlooked by factuality-based measures. Experiments on three real-world datasets show that PURE consistently reduces preference-inconsistent explanations and factual hallucinations while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy, explanation quality, and inference efficiency. These results highlight that trustworthy explanations require not only factual correctness but also justification aligned with user preferences.

</details>


### [152] [Odin: Multi-Signal Graph Intelligence for Autonomous Discovery in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03097)
*Muyukani Kizito,Elizabeth Nyambere*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Odin是首个生产级部署的图智能引擎，用于自主发现知识图谱中有意义的模式，无需预先指定查询。它通过COMPASS评分整合结构重要性、语义合理性、时间相关性和社区感知指导，解决"回音室"问题，在医疗和保险等受监管行业成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于检索的系统需要预定义查询，无法自主发现知识图谱中的未知模式。现有方法容易陷入局部密集社区的"回音室"问题，且缺乏在受监管行业所需的可追溯性和可靠性。

Method: 提出COMPASS评分系统，整合四个信号：1) 基于个性化PageRank的结构重要性；2) 使用神经概率逻辑学习(NPLL)作为判别式过滤器的语义合理性；3) 可配置衰减的时间相关性；4) 通过GNN识别桥接实体和社区间亲和度分数的社区感知指导。采用波束搜索实现O(b·h)复杂度。

Result: Odin是首个在受监管生产环境(医疗和保险)部署的自主发现系统，相比穷举探索保持高召回率，显著提升模式发现质量和分析师效率，同时保持完整的溯源可追溯性。

Conclusion: Odin通过多信号集成特别是桥接评分机制，成功解决了图探索中的"回音室"问题，为受监管行业提供了可靠、可追溯的自主知识图谱发现解决方案，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: We present Odin, the first production-deployed graph intelligence engine for autonomous discovery of meaningful patterns in knowledge graphs without prior specification. Unlike retrieval-based systems that answer predefined queries, Odin guides exploration through the COMPASS (Composite Oriented Multi-signal Path Assessment) score, a novel metric that combines (1) structural importance via Personalized PageRank, (2) semantic plausibility through Neural Probabilistic Logic Learning (NPLL) used as a discriminative filter rather than generative model, (3) temporal relevance with configurable decay, and (4) community-aware guidance through GNN-identified bridge entities and inter-community affinity scores. This multi-signal integration, particularly the bridge scoring mechanism, addresses the "echo chamber" problem where graph exploration becomes trapped in dense local communities. We formalize the autonomous discovery problem, prove theoretical properties of our scoring function, and demonstrate that beam search with multi-signal guidance achieves $O(b \cdot h)$ complexity while maintaining high recall compared to exhaustive exploration. To our knowledge, Odin represents the first autonomous discovery system deployed in regulated production environments (healthcare and insurance), demonstrating significant improvements in pattern discovery quality and analyst efficiency. Our approach maintains complete provenance traceability -- a critical requirement for regulated industries where hallucination is unacceptable.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Task Completion: Revealing Corrupt Success in LLM Agents through Procedure-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03116)
*Hongliu Cao,Ilias Driouich,Eoin Thomas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Procedure-Aware Evaluation (PAE)框架，通过结构化观察评估LLM代理的过程一致性，发现现有基准测试中27-78%的成功案例实际上存在违规行为，并揭示了不同模型的特定失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理基准测试主要关注任务是否完成，而忽略了"如何完成"的过程质量。在高风险应用中，过程合规性和一致性至关重要，需要更全面的评估框架来检测隐藏的违规行为。

Method: 提出PAE框架，将代理过程形式化为结构化观察，暴露观察、沟通和执行之间的一致性关系。通过多维度评估（效用、效率、交互质量、过程完整性）和应用多维门控机制，分类排除腐败结果。

Result: 在tau-bench上评估发现：1) 各维度捕获非冗余失败模式；2) 27-78%的基准报告成功实际上是隐藏违规的"腐败成功"；3) 门控机制显著降低Pass^4率并影响模型排名；4) 不同模型有特定失败特征；5) 暴露基准设计中的结构缺陷。

Conclusion: 仅评估任务完成是不够的，过程质量评估至关重要。PAE框架能有效检测隐藏的违规行为，揭示模型特定失败模式，并暴露基准设计缺陷，为LLM代理的可靠部署提供了更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly adopted in high-stakes settings, but current benchmarks evaluate mainly whether a task was completed, not how. We introduce Procedure-Aware Evaluation (PAE), a framework that formalizes agent procedures as structured observations and exposes consistency relationships between what agents observe, communicate, and execute. PAE evaluates agents along complementary axes (Utility, Efficiency, Interaction Quality, Procedural Integrity) and applies multi-dimensional gating that categorically disqualifies corrupt outcomes. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLM agents on tau-bench yields findings at the axis, compliance, and benchmark levels. At the axis level, the dimensions capture non-redundant failure modes: utility masks reliability gaps, speed does not imply precision, and conciseness does not predict intent adherence. At the procedural compliance level, 27-78% of benchmark reported successes are corrupt successes concealing violations across interaction and integrity. Furthermore, gating substantially collapses Pass^4 rate and affects model rankings. The analysis of corrupt success cases reveals distinctive per-model failure signatures: GPT-5 spreads errors across policy, execution, and intent dimensions; Kimi-K2-Thinking concentrates 78% of violations in policy faithfulness and compliance; and Mistral-Large-3 is dominated by faithfulness failures. At the benchmark level, our analysis exposes structural flaws in the benchmark design, including task scope gaps, contradictory reward signals, and simulator artifacts that produce accidental successes.

</details>


### [154] [AI Space Physics: Constitutive boundary semantics for open AI institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03119)
*Oleg Romanchuk,Roman Bondar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AI空间物理学作为开放、自扩展AI机构的构成语义学，将权限边界扩展视为需要见证的一类边界事件，即使没有立即的外部效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理部署越来越像持久性机构而非一次性推理端点，它们积累状态、调用外部工具、协调多个运行时，并随时间修改其未来权限边界。现有治理语言通常指定决策层约束，但对边界跨越的因果机制定义不足，特别是那些不立即改变外部世界但扩展机构未来能力的过渡。

Method: 引入AI空间物理学作为构成语义学，定义包含类型化边界通道、视野受限可达语义和膜见证纪律的最小状态模型。核心法律家族(P-1, P-1a, P-1b, P-1c)要求见证完整性、非旁路调解、原子裁决到效果转换，以及可重放的裁决类重建。明确将二阶效应分为结构扩展和政策拓宽。

Result: 建立了将权限边界扩展重新分类为一类边界事件的精确语义框架，要求即使没有立即提交的外部变化，扩展过渡也保持裁决相关性，并具有构成性见证义务。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了精确而非扩展性的新颖性主张：将权限表面扩展重新分类为具有构成见证义务的一类边界事件，使得即使没有立即承诺的扩展也保持裁决相关性，为开放、自扩展AI机构提供了正式的治理语义学基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI deployments increasingly behave as persistent institutions rather than one-shot inference endpoints: they accumulate state, invoke external tools, coordinate multiple runtimes, and modify their future authority surface over time. Existing governance language typically specifies decision-layer constraints but leaves the causal mechanics of boundary crossing underdefined, particularly for transitions that do not immediately change the external world yet expand what the institution can later do.
  This paper introduces AI Space Physics as a constitutive semantics for open, self-expanding AI institutions. We define a minimal state model with typed boundary channels, horizon-limited reach semantics, and a membrane-witness discipline. The core law family (P-1, P-1a, P-1b, P-1c) requires witness completeness, non-bypass mediation, atomic adjudication-to-effect transitions, and replayable reconstruction of adjudication class. We explicitly separate second-order effects into structural expansion and policy broadening, and treat expansion transitions as governance-relevant even when immediate external deltas are zero.
  The novelty claim is precise rather than expansive: this work does not introduce mediation as a concept; it reclassifies authority-surface expansion as a first-class boundary event with constitutive witness obligations. In this semantics, expansion without immediate commit remains adjudication-relevant.

</details>


### [155] [Agentic AI-based Coverage Closure for Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03147)
*Sivaram Pothireddypalli,Ashish Raman,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于智能体AI的工作流，利用LLM驱动的生成式AI自动化形式验证的覆盖率分析、识别覆盖率缺口并生成所需的形式属性，加速验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成电路开发中，覆盖率闭合是关键要求，但传统穷举方法往往无法在项目时间内实现完全覆盖，需要更高效的自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用智能体AI驱动的工作流，利用LLM支持的生成式AI自动化形式验证的覆盖率分析，识别覆盖率缺口，并自动生成所需的形式属性。

Result: 在开源和内部设计上的基准测试显示覆盖率指标有显著提升，改进程度与设计复杂度相关，比较分析验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 智能体AI技术有潜力提高形式验证的生产力，支持全面的覆盖率闭合，为IC验证提供更高效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Coverage closure is a critical requirement in Integrated Chip (IC) development process and key metric for verification sign-off. However, traditional exhaustive approaches often fail to achieve full coverage within project timelines. This study presents an agentic AI-driven workflow that utilizes Large Language Model (LLM)-enabled Generative AI (GenAI) to automate coverage analysis for formal verification, identify coverage gaps, and generate the required formal properties. The framework accelerates verification efficiency by systematically addressing coverage holes. Benchmarking open-source and internal designs reveals a measurable increase in coverage metrics, with improvements correlated to the complexity of the design. Comparative analysis validates the effectiveness of this approach. These results highlight the potential of agentic AI-based techniques to improve formal verification productivity and support comprehensive coverage closure.

</details>


### [156] [Saarthi for AGI: Towards Domain-Specific General Intelligence for Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03175)
*Aman Kumar,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Luu Danh Minh,Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Sivaram Pothireddypalli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Saarthi是一个多智能体协作的端到端形式验证框架，通过引入结构化规则手册、规范语法和高级RAG技术，将断言生成准确率提升70%，覆盖收敛迭代次数减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体在处理形式验证等复杂任务时容易出现幻觉和错误，Saarthi框架虽然提供完整流程但仅有约40%的有效性，需要增强其鲁棒性和可靠性，向领域特定通用智能迈进。

Method: 提出两项关键改进：(1) 结构化规则手册和规范语法，提高SystemVerilog断言生成的准确性和可控性；(2) 集成GraphRAG等高级检索增强生成技术，为智能体提供技术知识和最佳实践，实现输出的迭代优化。

Result: 在NVIDIA CVDP基准测试中，生成的断言准确率提升70%，达到覆盖收敛所需的迭代次数减少50%，显著提升了框架的整体性能。

Conclusion: Saarthi通过结构化规则和高级RAG技术的增强，在处理需要短期、短上下文能力的领域（如形式验证）中展现出强大潜力，是向领域特定通用智能迈进的重要一步。

Abstract: Saarthi is an agentic AI framework that uses multi-agent collaboration to perform end-to-end formal verification. Even though the framework provides a complete flow from specification to coverage closure, with around 40% efficacy, there are several challenges that need to be addressed to make it more robust and reliable. Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is still a distant goal, and current Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are prone to hallucinations and making mistakes, especially when dealing with complex tasks such as formal verification. However, with the right enhancements and improvements, we believe that Saarthi can be a significant step towards achieving domain-specific general intelligence for formal verification. Especially for problems that require Short Term, Short Context (STSC) capabilities, such as formal verification, Saarthi can be a powerful tool to assist verification engineers in their work. In this paper, we present two key enhancements to the Saarthi framework: (1) a structured rulebook and specification grammar to improve the accuracy and controllability of SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, and (2) integration of advanced Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) techniques, such as GraphRAG, to provide agents with access to technical knowledge and best practices for iterative refinement and improvement of outputs. We also benchmark these enhancements for the overall Saarthi framework using challenging test cases from NVIDIA's CVDP benchmark targeting formal verification. Our benchmark results stand out with a 70% improvement in the accuracy of generated assertions, and a 50% reduction in the number of iterations required to achieve coverage closure.

</details>


### [157] [FEAST: Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Hierarchical Food Classification for the FoodEx2 System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03176)
*Lorenzo Molfetta,Alessio Cocchieri,Stefano Fantazzini,Giacomo Frisoni,Luca Ragazzi,Gianluca Moro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FEAST框架通过三阶段检索增强方法解决FoodEx2食品分类系统的复杂层级分类问题，在稀有类别上显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FoodEx2食品分类系统面临复杂标签依赖、数据稀疏和极端输出维度等挑战，现有模型无法有效处理其实践约束，需要专门解决方案

Method: 提出FEAST框架，将FoodEx2分类分解为三阶段：1)基础术语识别，2)多标签方面预测，3)方面描述符分配，利用系统层级结构指导训练并进行深度度量学习

Result: 在多语言FoodEx2基准测试中，FEAST在稀有类别上的F1分数比欧洲CNN基线提升12-38%

Conclusion: FEAST框架通过检索增强和深度度量学习有效解决了FoodEx2系统的复杂分类问题，特别是在数据稀疏和细粒度标签场景下表现出色

Abstract: Hierarchical text classification (HTC) and extreme multi-label classification (XML) tasks face compounded challenges from complex label interdependencies, data sparsity, and extreme output dimensions. These challenges are exemplified in the European Food Safety Authority's FoodEx2 system-a standardized food classification framework essential for food consumption monitoring and contaminant exposure assessment across Europe. FoodEx2 coding transforms natural language food descriptions into a set of codes from multiple standardized hierarchies, but faces implementation barriers due to its complex structure. Given a food description (e.g., "organic yogurt''), the system identifies its base term ("yogurt''), all the applicable facet categories (e.g., "production method''), and then, every relevant facet descriptors to each category (e.g., "organic production''). While existing models perform adequately on well-balanced and semantically dense hierarchies, no work has been applied on the practical constraints imposed by the FoodEx2 system. The limited literature addressing such real-world scenarios further compounds these challenges. We propose FEAST (Food Embedding And Semantic Taxonomy), a novel retrieval-augmented framework that decomposes FoodEx2 classification into a three-stage approach: (1) base term identification, (2) multi-label facet prediction, and (3) facet descriptor assignment. By leveraging the system's hierarchical structure to guide training and performing deep metric learning, FEASTlearns discriminative embeddings that mitigate data sparsity and improve generalization on rare and fine-grained labels. Evaluated on the multilingual FoodEx2 benchmark, FEAST outperforms the prior European's CNN baseline F1 scores by 12-38 % on rare classes.

</details>


### [158] [Neuro-Symbolic Artificial Intelligence: A Task-Directed Survey in the Black-Box Models Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03177)
*Giovanni Pio Delvecchio,Lorenzo Molfetta,Gianluca Moro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文探讨了神经符号方法在现实任务中的应用，分析了如何通过结合符号系统来增强可解释性和推理能力，为研究者提供资源参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号方法作为实现人类水平智能的可能途径之一，虽然具有推断或利用行为模式的能力，但在语义泛化性和处理复杂领域方面存在局限。随着2017年以来连接主义系统的突破性进展，神经符号方法的竞争力受到质疑，特别是在自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域。因此需要系统调查神经符号方法在特定任务中的进展。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，系统性地调查了神经符号领域在特定任务方面的进展，重点关注如何通过整合符号系统来增强可解释性和推理能力。研究提供了详细的复现信息和深度评论。

Result: 研究发现神经符号方法在增强系统可解释性和推理能力方面具有重要价值，特别是在需要透明决策和逻辑推理的现实任务中。研究结果为探索可解释神经符号方法提供了资源基础。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法仍然是实现可解释人工智能的重要途径，特别是在需要结合符号推理和神经学习的现实应用场景中。该综述为研究者提供了探索可解释神经符号方法的宝贵资源。

Abstract: The integration of symbolic computing with neural networks has intrigued researchers since the first theorizations of Artificial intelligence (AI). The ability of Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) methods to infer or exploit behavioral schema has been widely considered as one of the possible proxies for human-level intelligence. However, the limited semantic generalizability and the challenges in declining complex domains with pre-defined patterns and rules hinder their practical implementation in real-world scenarios. The unprecedented results achieved by connectionist systems since the last AI breakthrough in 2017 have raised questions about the competitiveness of NeSy solutions, with particular emphasis on the Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision fields. This survey examines task-specific advancements in the NeSy domain to explore how incorporating symbolic systems can enhance explainability and reasoning capabilities. Our findings are meant to serve as a resource for researchers exploring explainable NeSy methodologies for real-life tasks and applications. Reproducibility details and in-depth comments on each surveyed research work are made available at https://github.com/disi-unibo-nlp/task-oriented-neuro-symbolic.git.

</details>


### [159] [Expectation and Acoustic Neural Network Representations Enhance Music Identification from Brain Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03190)
*Shogo Noguchi,Taketo Akama,Tai Nakamura,Shun Minamikawa,Natalia Polouliakh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用ANN表示作为监督信号改进EEG音乐识别，区分声学和期望相关表示作为教师目标能提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐聆听时皮层活动编码声学和期望相关信息，ANN表示类似于皮层表示，可作为EEG识别的监督信号。研究探索如何通过区分不同类型的ANN表示作为教师目标来改进EEG音乐识别

Method: 使用人工神经网络（ANN）表示作为教师目标，区分声学相关表示和期望相关表示。模型预训练来预测这些表示，然后用于EEG音乐识别。比较单独使用每种表示、组合使用以及随机初始化基准的性能

Result: 预测声学或期望相关ANN表示的预训练模型优于非预训练基线。组合两种表示能产生互补增益，性能超过通过随机初始化形成的强种子集成。期望表示直接从原始信号计算，无需手动标签，反映超出起始点或音高的预测结构

Conclusion: 教师表示类型影响下游性能，表示学习可通过神经编码指导。该方法为预测性音乐认知和神经解码提供进展，期望表示能研究跨多样刺激的多层预测编码。其在大规模多样化数据集上的可扩展性表明有潜力开发基于皮层编码原理的通用EEG模型

Abstract: During music listening, cortical activity encodes both acoustic and expectation-related information. Prior work has shown that ANN representations resemble cortical representations and can serve as supervisory signals for EEG recognition. Here we show that distinguishing acoustic and expectation-related ANN representations as teacher targets improves EEG-based music identification. Models pretrained to predict either representation outperform non-pretrained baselines, and combining them yields complementary gains that exceed strong seed ensembles formed by varying random initializations. These findings show that teacher representation type shapes downstream performance and that representation learning can be guided by neural encoding. This work points toward advances in predictive music cognition and neural decoding. Our expectation representation, computed directly from raw signals without manual labels, reflects predictive structure beyond onset or pitch, enabling investigation of multilayer predictive encoding across diverse stimuli. Its scalability to large, diverse datasets further suggests potential for developing general-purpose EEG models grounded in cortical encoding principles.

</details>


### [160] [No Memorization, No Detection: Output Distribution-Based Contamination Detection in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03203)
*Omer Sela*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CDD方法通过测量模型输出分布的峰值来检测数据污染，但在小语言模型上其有效性取决于微调是否产生逐字记忆。低秩适配可以在不记忆的情况下学习污染数据，此时CDD检测效果接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究CDD（基于输出分布的数据污染检测方法）在小语言模型上的有效性和局限性，特别是在不同微调策略下的表现差异。

Method: 在70M到410M参数的小语言模型上进行控制性污染实验，使用GSM8K、HumanEval和MATH数据集，比较不同微调策略（特别是低秩适配与全参数微调）对CDD检测效果的影响。

Result: CDD的有效性关键取决于微调是否导致逐字记忆：低秩适配可以在不记忆的情况下学习污染数据，此时CDD检测准确率接近随机水平；只有当微调能力足够强、能诱导记忆时，CDD才能恢复高检测准确率。

Conclusion: 存在一个记忆阈值控制着污染的可检测性，参数高效的微调方法可能产生输出分布方法无法检测到的数据污染，这对实际应用有重要启示。

Abstract: CDD, or Contamination Detection via output Distribution, identifies data contamination by measuring the peakedness of a model's sampled outputs. We study the conditions under which this approach succeeds and fails on small language models ranging from 70M to 410M parameters. Using controlled contamination experiments on GSM8K, HumanEval, and MATH, we find that CDD's effectiveness depends critically on whether fine-tuning produces verbatim memorization. With low-rank adaptation, models can learn from contaminated data without memorizing it, and CDD performs at chance level even when the data is verifiably contaminated. Only when fine-tuning capacity is sufficient to induce memorization does CDD recover strong detection accuracy. Our results characterize a memorization threshold that governs detectability and highlight a practical consideration: parameter-efficient fine-tuning can produce contamination that output-distribution methods do not detect. Our code is available at https://github.com/Sela-Omer/Contamination-Detection-Small-LM

</details>


### [161] [NeuroSkill(tm): Proactive Real-Time Agentic System Capable of Modeling Human State of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03212)
*Nataliya Kosmyna,Eugene Hauptmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroSkill系统是一个实时主动代理系统，利用EXG基础模型和文本嵌入模型，通过脑机接口离线运行，能够建模人类心理状态并提供工具调用和协议执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统缺乏直接从脑机接口实时建模人类心理状态的能力，无法在边缘设备上离线运行并提供认知和情感层面的主动交互。

Method: 使用NeuroLoop自定义框架，结合EXG基础模型和文本嵌入模型，通过SKILL.md描述人类心理状态，从BCI设备获取生物物理和脑信号，实现代理流程管理。

Result: 开发出首个能够实时建模人类心理状态、在边缘设备上完全离线运行的主动代理系统，支持通过API和CLI进行交互，提供工具调用和协议执行功能。

Conclusion: NeuroSkill系统代表了脑机接口与人工智能代理系统融合的重要进展，通过GPLv3开源和AI100伦理许可，为认知增强和情感交互提供了新的技术框架。

Abstract: Real-time proactive agentic system, capable of modeling Human State of Mind, using foundation EXG model and text embeddings model, running fully offline on the edge. Unlike all previously known systems, the NeuroSkill(tm) system leverages SKILL.md description of Human's State of Mind via API and CLI provided by the system, directly from the Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) devices, which records Human biophysical and brain signals. Our custom harness - NeuroLoop(tm) - utilizes all of the above to run agentic flow that manages to engage with the Human on multiple cognitive and affective levels of their State of Mind (e.g., empathy), by providing actionable tool calls and protocol execution with explicit or implicit requests from the Human. GPLv3 open-source software with ethically aligned AI100 licensing for the skill markdown.

</details>


### [162] [AI-for-Science Low-code Platform with Bayesian Adversarial Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03233)
*Zihang Zeng,Jiaquan Zhang,Pengze Li,Yuan Qi,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个用于科学AI任务的贝叶斯对抗多智能体低代码平台，通过三个LLM智能体（任务管理器、代码生成器、评估器）在贝叶斯框架下协同工作，优化测试用例和代码生成，减少对LLM可靠性的依赖并解决科学任务评估不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化科学代码生成方面有潜力，但面临可靠性挑战、多智能体工作流中的错误传播问题，以及在成功指标不明确的领域中的评估困难。

Method: 设计了一个贝叶斯对抗多智能体框架，包含三个LLM智能体：任务管理器（将用户输入转化为可执行计划和自适应测试用例）、代码生成器（生成候选解决方案）、评估器（提供全面反馈）。采用对抗循环，任务管理器迭代优化测试用例挑战代码生成器，同时使用贝叶斯原理动态更新提示分布，整合代码质量指标（功能正确性、结构对齐、静态分析）。

Result: 基准评估显示LCP在生成稳健代码同时最小化错误传播方面有效。在地球科学跨学科任务测试中表现出强可靠性，优于竞争模型。

Conclusion: 提出的低代码平台通过贝叶斯对抗多智能体框架解决了科学AI任务中的LLM可靠性、错误传播和评估不确定性问题，同时简化了人机协作，无需非专业用户进行手动提示工程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate potentials for automating scientific code generation but face challenges in reliability, error propagation in multi-agent workflows, and evaluation in domains with ill-defined success metrics. We present a Bayesian adversarial multi-agent framework specifically designed for AI for Science (AI4S) tasks in the form of a Low-code Platform (LCP). Three LLM-based agents are coordinated under the Bayesian framework: a Task Manager that structures user inputs into actionable plans and adaptive test cases, a Code Generator that produces candidate solutions, and an Evaluator providing comprehensive feedback. The framework employs an adversarial loop where the Task Manager iteratively refines test cases to challenge the Code Generator, while prompt distributions are dynamically updated using Bayesian principles by integrating code quality metrics: functional correctness, structural alignment, and static analysis. This co-optimization of tests and code reduces dependence on LLM reliability and addresses evaluation uncertainty inherent to scientific tasks. LCP also streamlines human-AI collaboration by translating non-expert prompts into domain-specific requirements, bypassing the need for manual prompt engineering by practitioners without coding backgrounds. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate LCP's effectiveness in generating robust code while minimizing error propagation. The proposed platform is also tested on an Earth Science cross-disciplinary task and demonstrates strong reliability, outperforming competing models.

</details>


### [163] [Density-Guided Response Optimization: Community-Grounded Alignment via Implicit Acceptance Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03242)
*Patrick Gerard,Svitlana Volkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DGRO方法，利用社区接受行为在表示空间中的几何结构作为隐式偏好信号，无需显式偏好标签即可对齐语言模型到社区规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖显式偏好监督或预定义原则，这在资源匮乏、敏感话题或文化背景不同的在线社区中成本高昂、伦理困难或文化不匹配。社区通过接受、参与和保留内容的行为已经隐式表达了偏好。

Method: 提出密度引导响应优化(DGRO)：发现社区接受行为在表示空间中形成几何结构（接受内容占据连贯高密度区域，拒绝内容位于稀疏或不对齐区域），利用这种密度结构作为隐式偏好信号来对齐语言模型。

Result: 实验表明局部密度能恢复成对社区判断，几何结构编码了有意义的偏好信号。在缺乏标注的多样化社区中，DGRO对齐的模型在人类标注者、领域专家和模型评估中均优于监督和提示基线。

Conclusion: DGRO为缺乏显式偏好监督或显式监督与实际情况不匹配的社区提供了实用的对齐替代方案，并讨论了从涌现接受行为中学习的意义和风险。

Abstract: Language models deployed in online communities must adapt to norms that vary across social, cultural, and domain-specific contexts. Prior alignment approaches rely on explicit preference supervision or predefined principles, which are effective for well-resourced settings but exclude most online communities -- particularly those without institutional backing, annotation infrastructure, or organized around sensitive topics -- where preference elicitation is costly, ethically fraught, or culturally misaligned.
  We observe that communities already express preferences implicitly through what content they accept, engage with, and allow to persist. We show that this acceptance behavior induces measurable geometric structure in representation space: accepted responses occupy coherent, high-density regions that reflect community-specific norms, while rejected content falls in sparser or misaligned areas. We operationalize this structure as an implicit preference signal for alignment and introduce density-guided response optimization (DGRO), a method that aligns language models to community norms without requiring explicit preference labels.
  Using labeled preference data, we demonstrate that local density recovers pairwise community judgments, indicating that geometric structure encodes meaningful preference signal. We then apply DGRO in annotation-scarce settings across diverse communities spanning platform, topic, and language. DGRO-aligned models consistently produce responses preferred by human annotators, domain experts, and model-based judges over supervised and prompt-based baselines. We position DGRO as a practical alignment alternative for communities where explicit preference supervision is unavailable or misaligned with situated practices, and discuss the implications and risks of learning from emergent acceptance behavior.

</details>


### [164] [Valet: A Standardized Testbed of Traditional Imperfect-Information Card Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03252)
*Mark Goadrich,Achille Morenville,Éric Piette*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Valet是一个包含21个传统不完全信息纸牌游戏的多样化测试平台，用于标准化评估游戏AI算法在不同游戏中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI算法在不完全信息游戏中的评估通常基于单个游戏的性能指标，难以评估算法在不同游戏选择中的鲁棒性。纸牌游戏因其隐藏手牌和随机抽牌的特性，是研究不完全信息的天然领域。

Method: 1) 收集21个传统不完全信息纸牌游戏，涵盖多种类型、文化、玩家数量、牌组结构、机制、获胜条件以及信息隐藏和揭示方法；2) 使用RECYCLE卡牌游戏描述语言标准化每个游戏的规则实现；3) 通过随机模拟实证分析每个游戏的分支因子和持续时间；4) 报告蒙特卡洛树搜索玩家对抗随机对手的基准得分分布。

Result: 创建了Valet测试平台，包含21个多样化纸牌游戏，提供了标准化的游戏规则实现。通过实证分析获得了每个游戏的分支因子和持续时间特征，并建立了蒙特卡洛树搜索对抗随机对手的基准性能。

Conclusion: Valet作为一个全面且多样化的不完全信息纸牌游戏测试平台，能够促进游戏AI算法的比较研究，帮助评估算法在不同游戏环境中的鲁棒性，为不完全信息游戏系统的研究提供了标准化的基准测试套件。

Abstract: AI algorithms for imperfect-information games are typically compared using performance metrics on individual games, making it difficult to assess robustness across game choices. Card games are a natural domain for imperfect information due to hidden hands and stochastic draws. To facilitate comparative research on imperfect-information game-playing algorithms and game systems, we introduce Valet, a diverse and comprehensive testbed of 21 traditional imperfect-information card games. These games span multiple genres, cultures, player counts, deck structures, mechanics, winning conditions, and methods of hiding and revealing information. To standardize implementations across systems, we encode the rules of each game in RECYCLE, a card game description language. We empirically characterize each game's branching factor and duration using random simulations, reporting baseline score distributions for a Monte Carlo Tree Search player against random opponents to demonstrate the suitability of Valet as a benchmarking suite.

</details>


### [165] [Inherited Goal Drift: Contextual Pressure Can Undermine Agentic Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03258)
*Achyutha Menon,Magnus Saebo,Tyler Crosse,Spencer Gibson,Eyon Jang,Diogo Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现现代语言模型代理在长上下文任务中仍存在目标漂移问题，特别是当条件化于较弱代理的预填充轨迹时，只有GPT-5.1表现出持续的抗漂移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型作为代理在长上下文任务中的加速部署，需要深入理解目标漂移现象。虽然早期模型已显示易受漂移影响，但最新模型的漂移程度尚不清楚，需要更新评估。

Method: 在模拟股票交易环境中测试最先进模型，研究条件化于较弱代理预填充轨迹时的漂移行为。同时在新急诊室分诊环境中进行类比实验，验证结果的跨领域可迁移性。

Result: 现代模型在对抗压力下表现稳健，但这种稳健性是脆弱的：当条件化于较弱代理的轨迹时，多数模型会继承漂移。只有GPT-5.1在所有测试模型中保持一致的抗漂移能力。漂移行为在提示变体间不一致，与指令层次遵循行为相关性差。

Conclusion: 现代语言模型代理仍易受上下文压力影响，需要更精细的后训练技术来缓解目标漂移问题。研究结果强调了跨不同领域评估代理鲁棒性的重要性。

Abstract: The accelerating adoption of language models (LMs) as agents for deployment in long-context tasks motivates a thorough understanding of goal drift: agents' tendency to deviate from an original objective. While prior-generation language model agents have been shown to be susceptible to drift, the extent to which drift affects more recent models remains unclear. In this work, we provide an updated characterization of the extent and causes of goal drift. We investigate drift in state-of-the-art models within a simulated stock-trading environment (Arike et al., 2025). These models are largely shown to be robust even when subjected to adversarial pressure. We show, however, that this robustness is brittle: across multiple settings, the same models often inherit drift when conditioned on prefilled trajectories from weaker agents. The extent of conditioning-induced drift varies significantly by model family, with only GPT-5.1 maintaining consistent resilience among tested models. We find that drift behavior is inconsistent between prompt variations and correlates poorly with instruction hierarchy following behavior, with strong hierarchy following failing to reliably predict resistance to drift. Finally, we run analogous experiments in a new emergency room triage environment to show preliminary evidence for the transferability of our results across qualitatively different settings. Our findings underscore the continued vulnerability of modern LM agents to contextual pressures and the need for refined post-training techniques to mitigate this.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for ISAC-Enabled Robotic Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02291)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向目标的语义通信框架，用于无人机避障任务，通过卡尔曼滤波预测位置、基于马氏距离的动态窗口方法生成控制信号，以及基于信息价值的深度Q网络优化传输，显著减少信号传输需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISAC框架在每个时隙传输感知和控制信号效率低下，需要更智能的通信框架来减少传输开销同时保证无人机避障任务的成功率。

Method: 1) 使用卡尔曼滤波器连续预测无人机位置，减少对连续感知信号传输的依赖；2) 基于马氏距离的动态窗口方法生成精确控制信号；3) 提出基于信息价值的深度Q网络决定何时传输感知和控制信号。

Result: 相比传统ISAC框架，GOSC框架在保持100%任务成功率的同时，将传输的感知信号数量减少92.4%，控制信号数量减少92.4%，传输时隙减少85.5%。

Conclusion: 提出的面向目标的语义通信框架能够显著提高ISAC系统的传输效率，通过智能选择有价值的信息进行传输，在保证任务性能的同时大幅降低通信开销。

Abstract: We investigate an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled BS for the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) obstacle avoidance task, and propose a goal-oriented semantic communication (GOSC) framework for the BS to transmit sensing and command and control (C&C) signals efficiently and effectively. Our GOSC framework establishes a closed loop for sensing-C&C generation-sensing and C&C transmission: For sensing, a Kalman filter (KF) is applied to continuously predict UAV positions, mitigating the reliance of UAV position acquisition on continuous sensing signal transmission, and enhancing position estimation accuracy through sensing-prediction fusion. Based on the refined estimation position provided by the KF, we develop a Mahalanobis distance-based dynamic window approach (MD-DWA) to generate precise C&C signals under uncertainty, in which we derive the mathematical expression of the minimum Mahalanobis distance required to guarantee collision avoidance. Finally, for efficient sensing and C&C signal transmission, we propose an effectiveness-aware deep Q-network (E-DQN) to determine the transmission of sensing and C&C signals based on their value of information (VoI). The VoI of sensing signals is quantified by the reduction in uncertainty entropy of UAV's position estimation, while the VoI of C&C signals is measured by their contribution to UAV navigation improvement. Extensive simulations validate the effectiveness of our proposed GOSC framework. Compared to the conventional ISAC transmission framework that transmits sensing and C&C signals at every time slot, GOSC achieves the same 100% task success rate while reducing the number of transmitted sensing and C&C signals by 92.4% and the number of transmission time slots by 85.5%.

</details>


### [167] [Safe Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation via Combined Model and Learning-based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02443)
*Alexander Schperberg,Yeping Wang,Stefano Di Cairano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合模型导纳控制（机械臂）与强化学习（腿部运动）的全身控制器，用于四足机器人实现安全、柔顺的物理交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调腿部运动与机械臂操作，同时考虑接触交互时的安全性和柔顺性仍然具有挑战性，需要一种能够实现全身协调控制的解决方案

Method: 1) 机械臂采用基于模型的导纳控制，将外部力矩映射为期望末端速度；2) 腿部运动使用强化学习策略；3) 通过参考调节器确保安全保证；4) 使用神经网络增强的卡尔曼滤波器进行基座速度估计

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人（配备6自由度机械臂和腕部力/力矩传感器）的仿真和硬件实验中验证了方法，实现了精确的交互驱动速度跟踪、柔顺行为以及在动态环境中的安全可靠性能

Conclusion: 提出的混合控制器成功实现了四足机器人的全身协调控制，能够在物理交互中提供柔顺响应和安全保证，为动态环境中的机器人操作提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Simultaneous locomotion and manipulation enables robots to interact with their environment beyond the constraints of a fixed base. However, coordinating legged locomotion with arm manipulation, while considering safety and compliance during contact interaction remains challenging. To this end, we propose a whole-body controller that combines a model-based admittance control for the manipulator arm with a Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy for legged locomotion. The admittance controller maps external wrenches--such as those applied by a human during physical interaction--into desired end-effector velocities, allowing for compliant behavior. The velocities are tracked jointly by the arm and leg controllers, enabling a unified 6-DoF force response. The model-based design permits accurate force control and safety guarantees via a Reference Governor (RG), while robustness is further improved by a Kalman filter enhanced with neural networks for reliable base velocity estimation. We validate our approach in both simulation and hardware using the Unitree Go2 quadruped robot with a 6-DoF arm and wrist-mounted 6-DoF Force/Torque sensor. Results demonstrate accurate tracking of interaction-driven velocities, compliant behavior, and safe, reliable performance in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Therapist Policy from Therapist-Exoskeleton-Patient Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02458)
*Grayson Snyder,Lorenzo Vianello,Levi Hargrove,Matthew L. Elwin,Jose Pons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出患者-治疗师力场（PTFF）可视化治疗师对患者运动学的响应，以及合成治疗师（ST）机器学习模型，通过机器人介导的物理交互治疗支持治疗师工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中风后康复治疗通常需要患者恢复正常行走步态，但传统治疗过程对治疗师来说非常耗费体力，可能导致治疗强度、持续时间和一致性随时间下降。

Method: PTFF使用变分自编码器（VAE）将患者和治疗师的步态运动学编码到共享低维潜在流形，通过高斯混合模型（GMM）学习从患者潜在状态到治疗师响应的概率向量场。ST采用长短期记忆（LSTM）网络，基于患者-治疗师交互数据预测治疗师施加的关节扭矩。

Result: 在8名中风后患者上使用留一法交叉验证进行训练和验证，模型集成到ROS外骨骼控制器中，能够基于预测的治疗师响应生成实时扭矩辅助。离线结果和初步测试表明其作为中风后外骨骼治疗替代方法的潜力。

Conclusion: PTFF帮助理解治疗师的行为，而ST将人类治疗师从外骨骼中解放出来，使他们能够持续监测患者的细微状况，为机器人介导的物理交互治疗提供了新方法。

Abstract: Post-stroke rehabilitation is often necessary for patients to regain proper walking gait. However, the typical therapy process can be exhausting and physically demanding for therapists, potentially reducing therapy intensity, duration, and consistency over time. We propose a Patient-Therapist Force Field (PTFF) to visualize therapist responses to patient kinematics and a Synthetic Therapist (ST) machine learning model to support the therapist in dyadic robot-mediated physical interaction therapy. The first encodes patient and therapist stride kinematics into a shared low-dimensional latent manifold using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) and models their interaction through a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), which learns a probabilistic vector field mapping patient latent states to therapist responses. This representation visualizes patient-therapist interaction dynamics to inform therapy strategies and robot controller design. The latter is implemented as a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network trained on patient-therapist interaction data to predict therapist-applied joint torques from patient kinematics. Trained and validated using leave-one-out cross-validation across eight post-stroke patients, the model was integrated into a ROS-based exoskeleton controller to generate real-time torque assistance based on predicted therapist responses. Offline results and preliminary testing indicate the potential of their use as an alternative approach to post-stroke exoskeleton therapy. The PTFF provides understanding of the therapist's actions while the ST frees the human therapist from the exoskeleton, allowing them to continuously monitor the patient's nuanced condition.

</details>


### [169] [A Novel Modular Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm with Multi-Segment Reconfigurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02468)
*Moeen Ul Islam,Cheng Ouyang,Xinda Qi,Azlan Zahid,Xiaobo Tan,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化、缆绳驱动的软体机械臂，具有多段可重构性，通过堆叠模块可扩展工作空间，并研究了硅胶刚度对性能的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发可重构、可扩展的软体机械臂系统，以适应不同的结构要求和应用需求，同时探索材料刚度对软体机器人性能的影响

Method: 采用模块化设计，使用软硅胶材料制造，嵌入肌腱导向通道和双螺旋保护结构，实现独立段控制，通过实验研究不同硅胶刚度对性能的影响

Result: 三段配置相比单段实现了平面工作空间面积13倍增加和体积38.9倍增加；软硅胶提高弯曲灵活性，硬硅胶增强结构刚度和负载稳定性

Conclusion: 模块化堆叠显著扩展工作空间，刚度调节可在柔顺性和强度之间取得平衡，为可配置、可重构的软体机械臂提供了有效设计方法

Abstract: This paper presents a novel, modular, cable-driven soft robotic arm featuring multi-segment reconfigurability. The proposed architecture enables a stackable system with independent segment control, allowing scalable adaptation to diverse structural and application requirements. The system is fabricated from soft silicone material and incorporates embedded tendon-routing channels with a protective dual-helical tendon structure. Experimental results showed that modular stacking substantially expanded the reachable workspace: relative to the single-segment arm, the three-segment configuration achieved up to a 13-fold increase in planar workspace area and a 38.9-fold increase in workspace volume. Furthermore, this study investigated the effect of silicone stiffness on actuator performance. The results revealed a clear trade-off between compliance and stiffness: softer silicone improved bending flexibility, while stiffer silicone improved structural rigidity and load-bearing stability. These results highlight the potential of stiffness tuning to balance compliance and strength for configuring scalable, reconfigurable soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [170] [COLREGs Compliant Collision Avoidance and Grounding Prevention for Autonomous Marine Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02484)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于海上自主水面舰船的统一运动规划方法，实现实时避碰、遵守国际海上避碰规则和防搁浅


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上自主水面舰船(MASS)在现实环境中的可靠部署面临挑战，特别是在拥挤水域。需要能够应对动态海事条件的安全且符合法规的运动规划策略

Method: 采用凸优化方法，整合基于速度障碍的碰撞约束、基于COLREGs的航向约束和基于水深测量的搁浅约束。扩展经典VO方法考虑目标船只位置和速度估计的不确定性，使用整数线性规划将非凸的浅水区域近似为凸几何形状

Result: 模拟结果表明，该方法在多船相遇场景中能够产生安全且符合法规的机动，适合实时自主海事导航

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够生成满足避碰、法规遵从、运动学限制和防搁浅要求的最优且动态可行的输入速度，为MASS提供有效的实时运动规划解决方案

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) are increasingly regarded as a promising solution to address crew shortages, improve navigational safety, and improve operational efficiency in the maritime industry. Nevertheless, the reliable deployment of MASS in real-world environments remains a significant challenge, particularly in congested waters where the majority of maritime accidents occur. This emphasizes the need for safe and regulation-aware motion planning strategies for MASS that are capable of operating under dynamic maritime conditions. This paper presents a unified motion planning method for MASS that achieves real time collision avoidance, compliance with International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs), and grounding prevention. The proposed work introduces a convex optimization method that integrates velocity obstacle-based (VO) collision constraints, COLREGs-based directional constraints, and bathymetry-based grounding constraints to generate computationally efficient, rule-compliant optimal velocity selection. To enhance robustness, the classical VO method is extended to consider uncertainty in the position and velocity estimates of the target vessel. Unnavigable shallow water regions obtained from bathymetric data, which are inherently nonconvex, are approximated via convex geometries using a integer linear programming (ILP), allowing grounding constraints to be incorporated into the motion planning. The resulting optimization generates optimal and dynamically feasible input velocities that meet collision avoidance, regulatory compliance, kinodynamic limits, and grounding prevention requirements. Simulation results involving multi-vessel encounters demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in producing safe and regulation-compliant maneuvers, highlighting the suitability of the proposed approach for real time autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [171] [A Robust Simulation Framework for Verification and Validation of Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Adverse Weather and Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02487)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Anthony S. Saaiby,JiaChang Xing,Keliang Pan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Heonyong Kang,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个增强的虚拟仿真框架，用于支持海事自主水面船舶在各种环境条件下的验证与确认，特别关注天气和海底地形对自主导航性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事自主水面船舶（MASS）虽然能提升航行安全、运营效率和成本效益，但其可靠部署需要在各种环境条件下进行严格的验证与确认，包括极端和安全关键场景。当前缺乏能够模拟真实海事环境（特别是恶劣天气和复杂海底地形）的综合仿真框架。

Method: 开发了一个增强的虚拟仿真框架，包含：1）高保真环境建模套件，能够模拟雨、雾、波浪等恶劣天气条件；2）参数化天气因素（如降雨和能见度）对海况特征、感知和传感系统的影响；3）集成美国主要港口的高分辨率海底地形数据，实现深度感知导航、搁浅预防和浅水/狭窄水道可控性评估。

Result: 该框架提供了广泛的配置能力，能够系统测试各种海事条件，包括在现实试验中不切实际或不安全的场景，从而支持MASS的验证与确认。

Conclusion: 提出的增强虚拟仿真框架能够有效支持海事自主水面船舶在各种真实环境条件下的验证与确认，特别通过模拟恶劣天气和复杂海底地形的影响，为MASS的安全可靠部署提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) have emerged as a promising solution to enhance navigational safety, operational efficiency, and long-term cost effectiveness. However, their reliable deployment requires rigorous verification and validation (V\&V) under various environmental conditions, including extreme and safety-critical scenarios. This paper presents an enhanced virtual simulation framework to support the V\&V of MASS in realistic maritime environments, with particular emphasis on the influence of weather and bathymetry on autonomous navigation performance. The framework incorporates a high-fidelity environmental modeling suite capable of simulating adverse weather conditions such as rain, fog, and wave dynamics. The key factors that affect weather, such as rain and visibility, are parameterized to affect sea-state characteristics, perception, and sensing systems, resulting in position and velocity uncertainty, reduced visibility, and degraded situational awareness. Furthermore, high-resolution bathymetric data from major U.S. ports are integrated to enable depth-aware navigation, grounding prevention capabilities, and evaluation of vessel controllability in shallow or confined waterways. The proposed framework offers extensive configurability, enabling systematic testing in a wide spectrum of maritime conditions, including scenarios that are impractical or unsafe to replicate in real-world trials, thus supporting the V\&V of MASS.

</details>


### [172] [Instant and Reversible Adhesive-free Bonding Between Silicones and Glossy Papers for Soft Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02500)
*Takumi Shibuya,Kazuya Murakami,Akitsu Shigetou,Jun Shintake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无粘合剂、溶剂可逆的硅胶-光面纸快速粘接技术，通过接触形成强界面，可承受高机械载荷，乙醇浸泡可完全分离并重复使用，用于软体机器人快速原型制作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硅胶与非延展材料集成方法依赖不可逆粘合剂或嵌入工艺，劳动密集且难以修改，需要一种快速、可逆、可重复使用的粘接方案。

Method: 开发硅胶-光面纸粘接技术，通过接触形成界面，无需粘合剂，使用乙醇浸泡实现可逆分离，研究表面官能团和硅胶模量对粘接的影响机制。

Result: SGB界面剪切强度>61kPa，可完全分离并重新组装且性能不损失，粘接机制为氢键和低聚物扩散协同作用，制造的软体驱动器性能优于传统嵌入层设计。

Conclusion: SGB技术简化了软体机器人制造过程，支持组件重用和快速迭代，为快速原型制作提供了可扩展、可持续的平台。

Abstract: Integrating silicone with non-extensible materials is a common strategy used in the fabrication of fluidically-driven soft actuators, yet conventional approaches often rely on irreversible adhesives or embedding processes that are labor-intensive and difficult to modify. This work presents silicone-glossy paper bonding (SGB), a rapid, adhesive-free, and solvent-reversible bonding approach that forms robust silicone-paper interfaces simply through contact. The SGB interface withstands high mechanical loads (shear strength > 61 kPa) and can be fully detached and reassembled via ethanol immersion without loss of performance, enabling component reuse and rapid redesign. Characterization studies indicate that surface functional groups primarily govern adhesion on the glossy paper and the modulus of the silicone, while durability and environmental response clarify the conditions for reversible debonding. The results further suggest a synergistic interaction of hydrogen bonding and oligomer diffusion, yielding strong yet reconfigurable adhesion. Soft actuators fabricated using SGB design exhibit equal or greater performance compared to conventional embedded-layer design and enable programmable actuation modes, including contraction, bending, and twisting. By simplifying fabrication while supporting reuse and rapid iteration, SGB offers a scalable and sustainable platform for rapid prototyping in soft robotics.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Object-Centric Spatial Reasoning for Sequential Manipulation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02511)
*Chrisantus Eze,Ryan C Julian,Christopher Crick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Unveiler是一个用于密集杂乱环境中机器人操作的解耦框架，将高层空间推理与底层动作执行分离，通过轻量级空间关系编码器和旋转不变动作解码器实现高效目标检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模端到端模型在密集杂乱环境中的物体检索任务中缺乏数据效率和模块化，需要更专业、解耦的系统来提高性能。

Method: 提出Unveiler框架，包含基于transformer的空间关系编码器（SRE）和旋转不变动作解码器。SRE通过两阶段训练：从启发式演示进行模仿学习初始化，然后用PPO微调。

Result: 在模拟环境中，部分遮挡场景成功率97.6%，完全遮挡场景成功率90.0%，优于传统端到端策略和现代大模型基线。SRE的空间推理能力可零样本迁移到真实场景。

Conclusion: 专业化解耦架构在复杂操作任务中具有优势，展示了对象中心推理的有效性，且无需重新训练即可在真实机器人上部署。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in cluttered environments presents a critical challenge for automation. Recent large-scale, end-to-end models demonstrate impressive capabilities but often lack the data efficiency and modularity required for retrieving objects in dense clutter. In this work, we argue for a paradigm of specialized, decoupled systems and present Unveiler, a framework that explicitly separates high-level spatial reasoning from low-level action execution. Unveiler's core is a lightweight, transformer-based Spatial Relationship Encoder (SRE) that sequentially identifies the most critical obstacle for removal. This discrete decision is then passed to a rotation-invariant Action Decoder for execution. We demonstrate that this decoupled architecture is not only more computationally efficient in terms of parameter count and inference time, but also significantly outperforms both classic end-to-end policies and modern, large-model-based baselines in retrieving targets from dense clutter. The SRE is trained in two stages: imitation learning from heuristic demonstrations provides sample-efficient initialization, after which PPO fine-tuning enables the policy to discover removal strategies that surpass the heuristic in dense clutter. Our results, achieving up to 97.6\% success in partially occluded and 90.0\% in fully occluded scenarios in simulation, make a case for the power of specialized, object-centric reasoning in complex manipulation tasks. Additionally, we demonstrate that the SRE's spatial reasoning transfers zero-shot to real scenes, and validate the full system on a physical robot requiring only geometric workspace calibration; no learned components are retrained.

</details>


### [174] [PathSpace: Rapid continuous map approximation for efficient SLAM using B-Splines in constrained environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02538)
*Aduen Benjumea,Andrew Bradley,Alexander Rast,Matthias Rolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathSpace：一种基于连续B样条的语义SLAM框架，用于紧凑表示环境并保持概率推理所需的连续概率密度函数，在自动驾驶赛车场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义SLAM技术主要依赖密集几何表示，限制了基于上下文约束的应用能力。需要更紧凑的环境表示方法，同时保持概率推理所需的连续概率密度函数。

Method: 提出PathSpace框架，使用连续B样条以紧凑方式表示环境，同时维护和推理连续概率密度函数。B样条的多重优势被应用于SLAM中，用于插值和拟合原本离散稀疏的环境。

Result: 在自动驾驶赛车场景中测试，利用预定义的赛道特征，相比传统地标方法，在保持相当精度水平的同时显著减少了表示复杂度，展示了在最小精度损失下限制系统资源使用的潜力。

Conclusion: PathSpace框架通过B样条表示提供了一种紧凑且概率完整的语义SLAM方法，在资源受限场景下具有实际应用价值，特别是在利用环境先验知识的领域如自动驾驶赛车。

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) plays a crucial role in enabling autonomous vehicles to navigate previously unknown environments.
  Semantic SLAM mostly extends visual SLAM, leveraging the higher density information available to reason about the environment in a more human-like manner. This allows for better decision making by exploiting prior structural knowledge of the environment, usually in the form of labels. Current semantic SLAM techniques still mostly rely on a dense geometric representation of the environment, limiting their ability to apply constraints based on context. We propose PathSpace, a novel semantic SLAM framework that uses continuous B-splines to represent the environment in a compact manner, while also maintaining and reasoning through the continuous probability density functions required for probabilistic reasoning. This system applies the multiple strengths of B-splines in the context of SLAM to interpolate and fit otherwise discrete sparse environments. We test this framework in the context of autonomous racing, where we exploit pre-specified track characteristics to produce significantly reduced representations at comparable levels of accuracy to traditional landmark based methods and demonstrate its potential in limiting the resources used by a system with minimal accuracy loss.

</details>


### [175] [Give me scissors: Collision-Free Dual-Arm Surgical Assistive Robot for Instrument Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02553)
*Xuejin Luo,Shiquan Sun,Runshi Zhang,Ruizhi Zhang,Junchen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型和实时障碍感知的双臂手术辅助机器人，能够根据外科医生指令零样本生成抓取和递送轨迹，在动态环境中实现无碰撞手术器械递送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手术中洗手护士需要频繁递送手术器械，导致身体疲劳和注意力下降。现有机器人洗手护士依赖预定义路径，在动态环境中泛化性差且存在安全风险。

Method: 使用视觉语言模型根据外科医生指令零样本生成机器人抓取和递送轨迹；提出实时障碍最小距离感知方法，并将其集成到统一二次规划框架中，确保双臂机器人在动态环境中自主运动时的反应性避障和自碰撞预防。

Result: 实验验证表明，所提出的机器人系统在手术器械递送任务中达到83.33%的成功率，并在所有试验中保持平滑、无碰撞的运动。

Conclusion: 该研究开发了一种能够安全、自主递送手术器械的双臂机器人系统，通过结合视觉语言模型和实时避障技术，解决了现有机器人洗手护士在动态环境中的局限性。

Abstract: During surgery, scrub nurses are required to frequently deliver surgical instruments to surgeons, which can lead to physical fatigue and decreased focus. Robotic scrub nurses provide a promising solution that can replace repetitive tasks and enhance efficiency. Existing research on robotic scrub nurses relies on predefined paths for instrument delivery, which limits their generalizability and poses safety risks in dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we present a collision-free dual-arm surgical assistive robot capable of performing instrument delivery. A vision-language model is utilized to automatically generate the robot's grasping and delivery trajectories in a zero-shot manner based on surgeons' instructions. A real-time obstacle minimum distance perception method is proposed and integrated into a unified quadratic programming framework. This framework ensures reactive obstacle avoidance and self-collision prevention during the dual-arm robot's autonomous movement in dynamic environments. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that the proposed robotic system achieves an 83.33% success rate in surgical instrument delivery while maintaining smooth, collision-free movement throughout all trials. The project page and source code are available at https://give-me-scissors.github.io/.

</details>


### [176] [Tensegrity Robot Endcap-Ground Contact Estimation with Symmetry-aware Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02596)
*Wenzhe Tong,Yicheng Jiang,Chi Zhang,Maani Ghaffari,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Sym-HGNN网络，利用机器人D3对称性从本体感知数据推断接触状态，结合InEKF提升张拉整体机器人位姿估计精度，仅需20%训练数据即可超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人具有轻量化和弹性结构，但由于其柔性和分布式地面接触，状态估计面临重大挑战。传统方法需要专用接触传感器，而本文旨在仅使用本体感知测量（IMU和缆索长度历史）实现准确状态估计。

Method: 提出对称感知异构图神经网络（Sym-HGNN），将机器人的D3二面对称性融入消息传递过程，直接从本体感知数据推断接触状态。然后将预测的接触状态集成到最先进的接触辅助不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（InEKF）中，用于改进位姿估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与CNN和MI-HGNN基线相比，该方法仅使用20%的训练数据即可实现高达15%的更高精度和5%的更高F1分数，同时保持与地面真实接触相当的低漂移和物理一致的状态估计结果。

Conclusion: 这项工作凸显了完全本体感知在张拉整体机器人中实现准确和鲁棒状态估计的潜力，提出的Sym-HGNN方法在样本效率和泛化能力方面表现出色。

Abstract: Tensegrity robots possess lightweight and resilient structures but present significant challenges for state estimation due to compliant and distributed ground contacts. This paper introduces a symmetry-aware heterogeneous graph neural network (Sym-HGNN) that infers contact states directly from proprioceptive measurements, including IMU and cable-length histories, without dedicated contact sensors. The network incorporates the robot's dihedral symmetry $D_3$ into the message-passing process to enhance sample efficiency and generalization. The predicted contacts are integrated into a state-of-the-art contact-aided invariant extended Kalman filter (InEKF) for improved pose estimation. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves up to 15% higher accuracy and 5% higher F1-score using only 20% of the training data compared to the CNN and MI-HGNN baselines, while maintaining low-drift and physically consistent state estimation results comparable to ground truth contacts. This work highlights the potential of fully proprioceptive sensing for accurate and robust state estimation in tensegrity robots. Code available at: https://github.com/Jonathan-Twz/Tensegrity-Sym-HGNN

</details>


### [177] [Wukong-Omni: Design, Modeling and Control of a Multi-mode Robot for Air, Land, and Underwater Exploration with All-in-One Propulsion Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02602)
*Yufan Liu,Rixi Yu,Junjie Li,Yishuai Zeng,Zhenting Wen,Cheng Li,Haifei Zhu,Shikang Lian,Wei Meng,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Wukong-Omni多模式机器人，采用统一推进系统实现陆地、空中和水下三域操作，通过创新机械设计重用电机并提升推力，实验显示推进效率提升100%，最大推力增加150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洪水灾害救援场景中，部分淹没的建筑物会阻止空中机器人进入较低楼层，限制了任务执行效果。需要一种能够在陆地、空中和水下多域操作的机器人来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出Wukong-Omni多模式机器人，采用创新机械设计实现统一推进系统，允许电机重用并改善推力生成。通过仿真和水槽实验优化效率和峰值推力，开发三域动态模型并提出统一的跨域控制框架。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比直接安装方法，推进效率提升100%，最大推力增加150%。综合实验验证了稳定的运动性能和流畅的跨域转换能力。户外实验进一步证明了在实际环境中的鲁棒性和适应性。

Conclusion: Wukong-Omni机器人通过统一推进系统和创新机械设计，成功实现了陆地、空中和水下三域操作，在洪水灾害救援等复杂场景中展现出强大的跨域适应能力。

Abstract: In flood disaster rescue scenarios, partially submerged buildings prevent aerial robots from accessing lower levels, limiting mission effectiveness. To address this challenge, this paper presents Wukong-Omni, a novel multimode robot capable of operating across land, air, and underwater using a unified propulsion system. The system is enabled by an innovative mechanical design that allows motor reuse and improves thrust generation. Efficiency and peak thrust are enhanced through simulation and tank-based optimization. Experimental results show a 100 percent improvement in propulsion efficiency and a 150 percent increase in maximum thrust compared with direct installation methods. Dynamic models for the three operating domains are developed, and a unified cross-domain control framework is proposed. Comprehensive experiments validate stable locomotion and smooth transition across domains. Outdoor experiments further demonstrate robustness and adaptability in real-world environments.

</details>


### [178] [Uni-Skill: Building Self-Evolving Skill Repository for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02623)
*Senwei Xie,Yuntian Zhang,Ruiping Wang,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-Skill是一个统一的技能中心框架，支持技能感知规划并促进自动技能演化，通过SkillFolder从大规模机器人视频中构建技能库，实现无需手动干预的离线技能检索和少样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能中心方法依赖固定的技能库，限制了在新任务中的适应性，需要手动干预。需要一种能够自动演化技能库、支持自适应规划的框架。

Method: 提出Uni-Skill框架，包含技能感知规划模块和SkillFolder技能库。SkillFolder从大规模非结构化机器人视频中构建层次化技能分类法，自动标注演示数据。当现有技能不足时，规划模块请求新技能实现，通过离线检索提供语义监督和空间轨迹参考。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，Uni-Skill相比现有VLM-based技能中心方法达到最先进性能，展示了先进的推理能力和强大的零样本泛化能力，能够处理广泛的新任务。

Conclusion: Uni-Skill通过自动技能演化和离线结构检索，将技能获取从低效的手动标注转变为高效的检索范式，实现了自适应规划和少样本技能推理，显著提升了组合任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: While skill-centric approaches leverage foundation models to enhance generalization in compositional tasks, they often rely on fixed skill libraries, limiting adaptability to new tasks without manual intervention. To address this, we propose Uni-Skill, a Unified Skill-centric framework that supports skill-aware planning and facilitates automatic skill evolution. Unlike prior methods that restrict planning to predefined skills, Uni-Skill requests for new skill implementations when existing ones are insufficient, ensuring adaptable planning with self-augmented skill library. To support automatic implementation of diverse skills requested by the planning module, we construct SkillFolder, a VerbNet-inspired repository derived from large-scale unstructured robotic videos. SkillFolder introduces a hierarchical skill taxonomy that captures diverse skill descriptions at multiple levels of abstraction. By populating this taxonomy with large-scale, automatically annotated demonstrations, Uni-Skill shifts the paradigm of skill acquisition from inefficient manual annotation to efficient offline structural retrieval. Retrieved examples provide semantic supervision over behavior patterns and fine-grained references for spatial trajectories, enabling few-shot skill inference without deployment-time demonstrations. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings verify the state-of-the-art performance of Uni-Skill over existing VLM-based skill-centric approaches, highlighting its advanced reasoning capabilities and strong zero-shot generalization across a wide range of novel tasks.

</details>


### [179] [cuNRTO: GPU-Accelerated Nonlinear Robust Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02642)
*Jiawei Wang,Arshiya Taj Abdul,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuNRTO框架通过两种动态优化架构（NRTO-DR和NRTO-FullADMM）在CUDA上实现鲁棒轨迹优化，利用并行SOCP投影和稀疏直接求解，在无人机、四旋翼和机械臂模型上实现最高139.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒轨迹优化对自主系统安全运行至关重要，但传统方法中的大规模二阶锥规划约束计算成本高昂，需要更高效的求解方案。

Method: 提出cuNRTO框架，包含两种架构：1) NRTO-DR使用Douglas-Rachford分裂法求解SOCP内子问题；2) NRTO-FullADMM利用ADMM方法进一步优化可扩展性。两者均通过自定义CUDA内核实现SOC投影和cuBLAS GEMM链更新反馈增益。

Result: 在单轮车、四旋翼飞行器和Franka机械臂模型上的仿真实验验证了cuNRTO的性能，实现了最高139.6倍的加速。

Conclusion: cuNRTO框架通过GPU并行化和新颖的优化架构，显著提升了鲁棒轨迹优化的计算效率，为实时自主系统决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robust trajectory optimization enables autonomous systems to operate safely under uncertainty by computing control policies that satisfy the constraints for all bounded disturbances. However, these problems often lead to large Second Order Conic Programming (SOCP) constraints, which are computationally expensive. In this work, we propose the CUDA Nonlinear Robust Trajectory Optimization (cuNRTO) framework by introducing two dynamic optimization architectures that have direct application to robust decision-making and are implemented on CUDA. The first architecture, NRTO-DR, leverages the Douglas-Rachford (DR) splitting method to solve the SOCP inner subproblems of NRTO, thereby significantly reducing the computational burden through parallel SOCP projections and sparse direct solves. The second architecture, NRTO-FullADMM, is a novel variant that further exploits the problem structure to improve scalability using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM). Finally, we provide GPU implementation of the proposed methodologies using custom CUDA kernels for SOC projection steps and cuBLAS GEMM chains for feedback gain updates. We validate the performance of cuNRTO through simulated experiments on unicycle, quadcopter, and Franka manipulator models, demonstrating speedup up to 139.6$\times$.

</details>


### [180] [Compositional Visual Planning via Inference-Time Diffusion Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02646)
*Yixin Zhang,Yunhao Luo,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Woo Chul Shin,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Tweedie估计边界一致性的组合扩散模型方法，用于长时程机器人规划，无需额外训练即可生成全局一致的计划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合方法在噪声数据空间中分解假设失效，导致全局计划不一致，需要更稳定的长时程规划方法

Method: 将长时程规划建模为重叠视频块链式因子图推理，在Tweedie估计上通过同步和异步消息传递强制边界一致性

Result: 相比现有基线有显著改进，能有效泛化到训练数据中未见过的起点-目标组合

Conclusion: 在干净数据估计上强制边界一致性是实现稳定组合生成的关键，无需额外训练即可提升长时程规划性能

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at short-horizon robot planning, yet scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging due to computational constraints and limited training data. Existing compositional approaches stitch together short segments by separately denoising each component and averaging overlapping regions. However, this suffers from instability as the factorization assumption breaks down in noisy data space, leading to inconsistent global plans. We propose that the key to stable compositional generation lies in enforcing boundary agreement on the estimated clean data (Tweedie estimates) rather than on noisy intermediate states. Our method formulates long-horizon planning as inference over a chain-structured factor graph of overlapping video chunks, where pretrained short-horizon video diffusion models provide local priors. At inference time, we enforce boundary agreement through a novel combination of synchronous and asynchronous message passing that operates on Tweedie estimates, producing globally consistent guidance without requiring additional training. Our training-free framework demonstrates significant improvements over existing baselines, effectively generalizing to unseen start-goal combinations that were not present in the original training data. Project website: https://comp-visual-planning.github.io/

</details>


### [181] [Watch Your Step: Learning Semantically-Guided Locomotion in Cluttered Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02657)
*Denan Liang,Yuan Zhu,Ruimeng Liu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemLoco是一个强化学习框架，通过语义地图和两阶段RL方法，让腿式机器人在密集杂乱环境中精确避障，特别是避免踩踏地面上的小型敏感物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在复杂地形上表现出色，但在杂乱环境中无法避免踩踏地面上的小型物体（如高成本设备或电缆）。这源于高层语义理解与底层控制的脱节，以及实际运行中高程地图的误差。

Method: 采用两阶段强化学习方法，结合软硬约束，进行像素级立足点安全推理。集成语义地图分配可通行性成本，而非仅依赖几何数据。

Result: 显著减少碰撞，提高敏感物体周围的安全性，在传统控制器可能造成损坏的情况下实现可靠导航。实验证明可有效应用于更复杂的非结构化真实环境。

Conclusion: SemLoco通过语义感知的强化学习框架，解决了腿式机器人在密集杂乱环境中精确避障的挑战，实现了比传统方法更安全的导航能力。

Abstract: Although legged robots demonstrate impressive mobility on rough terrain, using them safely in cluttered environments remains a challenge. A key issue is their inability to avoid stepping on low-lying objects, such as high-cost small devices or cables on flat ground. This limitation arises from a disconnection between high-level semantic understanding and low-level control, combined with errors in elevation maps during real-world operation. To address this, we introduce SemLoco, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework designed to avoid obstacles precisely in densely cluttered environments. SemLoco uses a two-stage RL approach that combines both soft and hard constraints and performs pixel-wise foothold safety inference, enabling more accurate foot placement. Additionally, SemLoco integrates a semantic map to assign traversability costs rather than relying solely on geometric data. SemLoco significantly reduces collisions and improves safety around sensitive objects, enabling reliable navigation in situations where traditional controllers would likely cause damage. Experimental results further demonstrate that SemLoco can be effectively applied to more complex, unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [182] [IMR-LLM: Industrial Multi-Robot Task Planning and Program Generation using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02669)
*Xiangyu Su,Juzhan Xu,Oliver van Kaick,Kai Xu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMR-LLM：基于大语言模型的工业多机器人任务规划与程序生成框架，通过构建析取图和确定性求解获得高效高层任务计划，再用过程树指导生成可执行低层程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中多机器人协作面临更严格的顺序约束和复杂依赖关系，现有LLM方法难以处理这些挑战，需要专门针对工业环境的多机器人协调框架。

Method: 提出IMR-LLM框架：1) 利用LLM辅助构建析取图，采用确定性求解方法获得可行高效的高层任务计划；2) 基于过程树指导LLM生成可执行的低层程序；3) 创建IMR-Bench基准测试集，包含三个复杂度级别的多机器人工业任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在所有评估指标上显著超越现有方法，证明其在工业多机器人任务规划中的有效性。

Conclusion: IMR-LLM框架成功解决了工业场景中多机器人协作的复杂约束问题，通过结合LLM的推理能力和确定性求解方法，实现了高效的任务规划和程序生成。

Abstract: In modern industrial production, multiple robots often collaborate to complete complex manufacturing tasks. Large language models (LLMs), with their strong reasoning capabilities, have shown potential in coordinating robots for simple household and manipulation tasks. However, in industrial scenarios, stricter sequential constraints and more complex dependencies within tasks present new challenges for LLMs. To address this, we propose IMR-LLM, a novel LLM-driven Industrial Multi-Robot task planning and program generation framework. Specifically, we utilize LLMs to assist in constructing disjunctive graphs and employ deterministic solving methods to obtain a feasible and efficient high-level task plan. Based on this, we use a process tree to guide LLMs to generate executable low-level programs. Additionally, we create IMR-Bench, a challenging benchmark that encompasses multi-robot industrial tasks across three levels of complexity. Experimental results indicate that our method significantly surpasses existing methods across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [183] [MMH-Planner: Multi-Mode Hybrid Trajectory Planning Method for UAV Efficient Flight Based on Real-Time Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02683)
*Yinghao Zhao,Chenguang Dai,Liang Lyu,Zhenchao Zhang,Chaozhen Lan,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实时环境感知的无人机多模式混合轨迹规划方法，通过动态选择最优规划模型来提高规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前运动规划算法由于策略不灵活和适应性弱，在规划效率方面存在局限，需要提高无人机在复杂环境中的自主操作能力。

Method: 1. 目标导向的空间感知方法快速评估飞行安全；2. 多模式混合轨迹规划机制基于先验空间感知选择最优规划模型；3. 惰性重规划策略仅在必要时触发重规划以减少计算资源消耗。

Result: 在仿真环境中进行的综合对比实验表明，该方法在多个指标上优于现有SOTA算法，特别是在平均规划迭代次数和每次迭代计算成本方面表现最佳。通过自主开发的智能无人机平台进行的实际飞行实验进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于实时环境感知的多模式混合轨迹规划方法能够有效提高无人机轨迹规划效率，在动态环境中实现高质量的自主飞行。

Abstract: Motion planning is a critical component of intelligent unmanned systems, enabling their complex autonomous operations. However, current planning algorithms still face limitations in planning efficiency due to inflexible strategies and weak adaptability. To address this, this paper proposes a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning method for UAVs based on real-time environmental awareness, which dynamically selects the optimal planning model for high-quality trajectory generation in response to environmental changes. First, we introduce a goal-oriented spatial awareness method that rapidly assesses flight safety in the upcoming environments. Second, a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning mechanism is proposed, which can enhance the planning efficiency by selecting the optimal planning model for trajectory generation based on prior spatial awareness. Finally, we design a lazy replanning strategy that triggers replanning only when necessary to reduce computational resource consumption while maintaining flight quality. To validate the performance of the proposed method, we conducted comprehensive comparative experiments in simulation environments. Results demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms across multiple metrics, achieving the best performance particularly in terms of the average number of planning iterations and computational cost per iteration. Furthermore, the effectiveness of our approach is further verified through real-world flight experiments integrated with a self-developed intelligent UAV platform.

</details>


### [184] [Robust Tightly-Coupled Filter-Based Monocular Visual-Inertial State Estimation and Graph-Based Evaluation for Autonomous Drone Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02742)
*Maulana Bisyir Azhari,Donghun Han,SungJun Park,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADR-VINS视觉惯性状态估计框架和ADR-FGO离线因子图优化框架，用于自主无人机竞速，在无GNSS环境中实现高效鲁棒的估计和性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机竞速需要计算高效且对极端速度和机动中感知退化具有鲁棒性的状态估计。传统方法依赖视觉惯性流水线，需要四个可见特征且存在信息损失，同时在无GNSS的竞赛环境中难以进行客观评估。

Method: 提出ADR-VINS：基于误差状态卡尔曼滤波的单目视觉惯性状态估计框架，直接使用门角特征像素重投影误差作为创新项，绕过PnP求解器，仅需两个可见角点，采用鲁棒重加权而非RANSAC处理异常值。同时提出ADR-FGO：离线因子图优化框架生成高保真参考轨迹。

Result: 在TII-RATM数据集上，ADR-VINS平均RMS平移误差0.134米，ADR-FGO作为平滑参考达到0.060米。在A2RL无人机锦标赛第二季中成功部署，在最高速度20.9米/秒的高机动飞行中保持稳定鲁棒估计。

Conclusion: ADR-VINS和ADR-FGO为自主无人机竞速提供了计算高效、鲁棒的状态估计解决方案，并在无GNSS环境中实现了有效的性能评估，在真实竞赛场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing (ADR) demands state estimation that is simultaneously computationally efficient and resilient to the perceptual degradation experienced during extreme velocity and maneuvers. Traditional frameworks typically rely on conventional visual-inertial pipelines with loosely-coupled gate-based Perspective-n-Points (PnP) corrections that suffer from a rigid requirement for four visible features and information loss in intermediate steps. Furthermore, the absence of GNSS and Motion Capture systems in uninstrumented, competitive racing environments makes the objective evaluation of such systems remarkably difficult. To address these limitations, we propose ADR-VINS, a robust, monocular visual-inertial state estimation framework based on an Error-State Kalman Filter (ESKF) tailored for autonomous drone racing. Our approach integrates direct pixel reprojection errors from gate corners features as innovation terms within the filter.
